  Q.13 [symb] H ≡ cognitive_system(a, [[a, φ_H]], [β_H]), a uniform
        -> [QUANTUM]  (Hadamard denotation on engine core)
  Q.14 [symb] S, T, Rz(λ): θ ← θ + Δθ, a unchanged
        -> [QUANTUM]  (phase gates are pure phase contributions)
  Q.15 [symb] CNOT: (a, θ)_i ← (a, θ)_{π_c(i)}
        -> [QUANTUM]  (control-conditioned basis-index permutation before the phase step)
  Q.16 [holds] max_i |q_i − a_i| = 1.11×10^-16
        = 1.11022 vs 1.11    -> [QUANTUM]
  Q.17 [holds] amplitudes² = [0.62246, 0.00000, 0.37754], zeros = [1]
        = 1.62246 vs 1.62246    -> [QUANTUM]
  Q.18 [symb] rule := [ "rule", name, head, pattern, denotation ], head ∈ {stmt, expr, postfix}
        -> [OTHER]  (grammar production stored as a ledger fact (×2))
  Q.19 [holds] unknown_prior(V)_i = 1/V; prepare_unknown(n) = uniform over 2^n
        = 0.125 vs 0.125    -> [QUANTUM, ENTROPY]
  Q.20 [symb] H(prior_{k+1}) < H(prior_k)
        -> [ENTROPY]  (entropy decreases monotonically as measurements commit)
  Q.21 [symb] estimate() = fold(update, unknown_prior(V), trace); forbidden() = zeros_of(estimate())
        -> [QUANTUM, ENTROPY]  (estimator folds over the commit trace)

NOTE: this is a speculative/fictional design derived from the papers' formulas;
it is a geometric visualisation, not an engineering-validated device.
